Squint 1.55.2
hotkey: ctrl+cmd+X (macOS) / ctrl+alt+X (Windows) - tap: snip, hold: see translated

Versions are MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH from here. The date-and-letter stamps below
were fine while the only person installing this was me: (ba) says nothing
about whether it is safe to skip, and "is (ax) older than (ba)" is not a
question anyone should have to work out. A number says what a release is.
  MAJOR - the app works differently enough that a habit has to change.
  MINOR - something new that was not there before.
  PATCH - something that was broken is not any more.
1.0.0 rather than 0.x because people other than me are running it daily,
which is what the number is actually about.

CHANGELOG.md is the user-facing list, one section per version, split into
Fixed / Added / Changed / Removed. It is where release notes come from -
the GitHub description and the panel in Settings both quote it, so there
is one copy to keep true. This file stays the engineering log: why a thing
was wrong, not only that it was.







1.55.2

  - Mac camera: _mac_needs_refresh() reads NSBundle.mainBundle for
    NSCameraUsageDescription before cv2 is touched; TCC kills the process
    otherwise (crash report 2026-10-22). Without it, "Restart Squint"
    spawns a detached helper that waits for this pid to exit, runs
    mac_bundle.rebuild() for each bundle from outside, and reopens the app.
  - Selfie check drawn in the hub: frames centre-cropped, mirrored,
    supersampled circular alpha mask, shown as a canvas image updated with
    itemconfigure; status and buttons in the panel.
---

1.55.1

  - Mac ring: no activateIgnoringOtherApps / focus_force on open (they
    brought Squint's main window up). The pointer is polled every 50 ms
    and fed to the same hover code, plus synthetic <Enter>/<Leave> for
    the widget under it - a borderless window of an inactive app gets no
    motion events. "Another app came to the front" now means a pid other
    than ours and the one that was in front at open. On close, if a click
    activated Squint, the previous app is brought back.
  - common.FlatButton (a Label) for buttons on macOS, where tk.Button is
    the native white button and ignores bg.
  - Selfie on Mac: OpenCV headless (requirements, darwin only, or fetched
    from the panel on first use), AVFoundation backend, retried while the
    camera permission prompt is up; NSCameraUsageDescription added to the
    bundle's Info.plist (rebuilt by mac_bundle on update).
---

1.55.0

  - Radial menu on macOS: hotkey registered on darwin too (Carbon,
    alt+space = Option+Space, same fallback). Ring window transparent with
    systemTransparent on Tk 8.6, dimmed on Tk 9; monitor geometry from
    NSScreen in points; NSApp activated on open; Escape and "another app
    came to the front" watched by polling (borderless windows on a Mac do
    not get key events reliably).
  - capture: region box in points on a Mac (mss grabs in points, returns
    pixels). StopBar keys via platform_util.key_is_down.
  - Finder selection through osascript; image clipboard through osascript
    «class PNGf»; nickname default from scutil ComputerName; selfie falls
    back to Photo Booth; 7-Zip's 7zz from 7z<ver>-mac.tar.xz; Video to MP4
    through avconvert when there is no FFmpeg.
---

1.54.1

  - Support line drawn on a white pill (polygon behind the text item).
  - Support view no longer pinned, so focus loss / an outside click closes
    the ring; hover still does not switch away from it.
  - qr.round_shapes / draw_round: dots (0.46 of a module) and rounded
    finder patterns on a white disc of radius 104 px, the code inscribed
    with a two-module margin. zbar decodes the user's PayPal URL and a
    transfer URL straight off the screen.
---

1.54.0

  - menu: selected starts as None (view "idle", panel hidden); keys and
    layout guard for it. Support is its own view: QR in the hub, a
    clickable line above the ring, panel hidden; a click on the QR returns
    to idle. Ring centre moved down 40 px when near the top edge to leave
    room for that line.
  - "Send" tool renamed "Transfers"; calendar panel 420 px wide, days on a
    uniform 7-column grid, larger fonts throughout.
---

1.53.0

  - hotkey_radial default alt+space; a saved ctrl+alt+space (1.50-1.52
    default) is moved unless hotkey_radial_chosen. If registering fails
    (1409: PowerToys Run), rebind to ctrl+alt+space. ctrl.radial_hotkey
    is shown in the hub.
  - ♥ now shows the user's PayPal p2p QR (decoded from the image he sent)
    in the hub, with Open PayPal in the panel.
  - send: nickname in radial.json (common.setting); device_name() uses it
    for discovery replies, offers and the phone page. Windows Firewall:
    firewall_ok() asks netsh for the "Squint" rule; allow_firewall() runs
    one elevated PowerShell that removes inbound Block rules for this
    Python (left by a dismissed Allow-access prompt - block wins over
    allow) and adds an Allow rule for pythonw/python, any profile.
---

1.52.0

  - menu.py rewritten around one panel (a Tk frame in a canvas window):
    views are list, hover (calendar: hidden 450 ms after the mouse has
    been on it and left), and pinned (cards, send, camera, city picker -
    no hover switching, no close on focus loss, Esc/✕ goes back). Ring
    drawings are tagged "ring" so redraws leave the panel alone.
  - card.py: Card(host=menu) renders into the panel; its timer runs on the
    root so work outlives the ring and is announced when done.
  - send.py: _share_view puts the QR in the hub (menu.set_qr) and the
    status, accept/decline and Open folder in the panel; the server stops
    when the view goes. nearby_cmd lists devices in the panel.
  - calendar_view.CalendarPanel; worldclock.py - zoneinfo when present,
    else built-in US/EU/AU/NZ rules, checked equal to zoneinfo every hour
    2025-2027 for all 41 cities.
  - capture.selfie: OpenCV (installed with RapidOCR) DirectShow capture on
    a thread, mirrored frames shown every 40 ms; falls back to the Windows
    Camera app without OpenCV.
  - ♥ in the hub, drawn as a polygon (font-independent).
---

1.51.0

  - app/radial/card.py: Card - settings (password, choice, check, note),
    Start, progress bar (fraction or indeterminate), Done / Open folder,
    or the error, all in one borderless window placed where the menu's
    panel was (menu passes `anchor`). Work runs on a thread and reports
    through progress(), which only stores values; the card polls them.
    The PDF, Zip, Convert and Extract commands, the first-use downloads
    and Send to a nearby device all go through it; ask_password /
    ask_choice / Progress are no longer used by them.
  - Scrolling capture: scroll_offset votes for an offset from rows that
    have something in them and accepts it when half of the overlap agrees,
    so a fixed header no longer makes every frame "not overlapping".
    Tested with 0, 80 and 200 px headers.
  - pick_files: "*.*" was read as the extension "." and nothing in the
    Explorer selection matched it.
---

1.50.2

  - Output folders: common.squint_dir(tool) = Downloads/Squint/<folder>
    (Screenshots, Recordings, PDF, Zip, Received, Converted, Extracted
    media); common.into() replaces beside(), which put results next to the
    input. tell() appends the folder for results inside it.
    Menu: the panel's height is measured from its wrapped note, and the
    hint line is drawn before the panel so the panel covers it.
---

1.50.1

  - Extract media: into Downloads/Squint/<name> - media (was beside the
    source file), Progress window driven by a per-file callback, and the
    folder opened when done.
    Progress windows were closed with root.after() from the worker
    thread; Tk is not safe there and it hung in testing. in_background()
    now takes the window and closes it on the Tk thread either way.
---

1.50.0

  - Radial tools menu (app/radial), Windows only for now: Ctrl+Alt+Space,
    registered beside the existing shortcuts (config hotkey_radial,
    radial_enabled). The hotkey again while recording stops the recording.
    menu.py - one borderless window over the monitor under the mouse,
    transparent colour key so only the ring and panel are drawn; losing
    focus closes it. Sized by the monitor's DPI.
    capture.py - region via snip.SnipOverlay; scrolling capture grabs the
    region every 220 ms and stitches by matching row signatures (48 px
    wide, 16 grey levels), so only rows the page actually scrolled by are
    added. Outline and stop bar use SetWindowDisplayAffinity so they are
    not in the capture.
    record.py - video through FFmpeg gdigrab (fetched on first use from
    BtbN's GitHub builds); GIF through mss + Pillow, 10 fps, one palette,
    hard stop at 6 s. Enter/Esc are watched with GetAsyncKeyState, not
    taken, so they still reach the app in front.
    docs.py - PDF tools on PyMuPDF (AES-256 when a password is given);
    Office to PDF through Office's own COM objects (LibreOffice if found);
    zip with the standard library, password zips through 7-Zip 7za.exe
    (fetched on first use from github.com/ip7z/7zip); media straight out
    of the OOXML zip (ppt/media, word/media) or PDF image xrefs.
    send.py - a small HTTP server per open window, keyed by a random token
    in the QR code; computers find each other by UDP broadcast on 53318
    and must accept an offer before a ticket to upload is issued. Uploads
    are raw bodies streamed to Downloads/Squint, names sanitised.
    qr.py - own QR encoder (byte mode, level M, versions 1-10) so no new
    package is needed; checked against zbar for every version.
    calendar_view.py - agenda.json in the data folder; reminders checked
    every 30 s.
    Files: what is selected in Explorer when the menu opened (Shell COM
    via PowerShell), otherwise a file dialog.
---

1.49.3

  - Watermark stamped across a page in a grid (a reader's name, a number
    and a date, at an angle, in rows and columns). Read with it, lines
    that crossed it came back broken and pieces of it were translated
    ("2.2", "2026. Guang-", "6 years, 0 months, 2 days").
    app/stamp.py: find_grid - autocorrelation of the edges (peaks, not
    hills), steps refined to the pixel at 1600 px, kept when strokes the
    same in nearly every copy cover >= 0.3% of the page and the same
    steps 27% longer find a third as much (text baselines and rules are
    "the same" at any step along them; a stamp only at its own).
    cleaned - those points painted with the background round them,
    unless they stand out more than the stamp does (1.15 x 1.6 x its
    median) in strokes, or are coloured. Only the copy that is read;
    the page shown is the original.
    ocr.image_to_lines looks for a grid while the page is read and reads
    again without the stamp when there is one. A snip is checked against
    the screen it was cut from (snip.py keeps it in the snip's info), as
    a snip may hold too little of the grid to see it repeat.
    Bench pages: none found. The user's page: found, stamp ~gone, text
    kept.
  - Online engine chosen for speed is waited for ONLINE_WAIT (3 s) at
    most, then the page is translated offline. Log: one page took 12 s
    offline (cause unknown), the speed learned from it sent the next
    pages online first, and an engine that failed there was tried line
    by line: 54 s and 105 s for pages the offline model then did in
    0.6 s and 1.9 s.
  - Offline speed: median of the last five pages (the faster of the
    first two), not a running average one slow page could move.
  - Updater: "CERTIFICATE_VERIFY_FAILED: unable to get local issuer
    certificate" on a friend's Windows PC (1.48.15). The updater used
    ssl.create_default_context() alone - the Windows store as it is,
    which lacks a public root until Windows fetches it for a browser.
    translator.ssl_context() now loads certifi and the system store
    together; updater._open tries it, then the system store, then the
    Mac keychain, moving on only for a certificate failure.
  - Gates r30 pass.

1.49.2

  - Photo pages: headlines in capitals came back untranslated (the engine
    took them for names). overlay._shouting / _sentence_case: sent in
    sentence case, the answer put back in capitals.
  - offline_model.single: a short label handed back unchanged is asked
    again as plain text - only 2+ letters and not starting lower case
    ("comune" -> "common", "た" -> "It's..." were worse than nothing).
  - main: overlay vs card decided by overlay.left_as_they_were (figures
    and codes such as "25-40PSI" no longer count as untranslated).
  - Covers on photos: overlay.soft_patch (blurred, feathered picture,
    ink light or dark to match); grey slabs gone. Drawn in ui_result and
    xray.
  - ocr.drop_stray_script: lines in another script sitting on a picture
    (CJK misreads of a photo) left out.
  - Layout on pictures ("pictured" lines, busy >= PATCH_BUSY): reader's box
    may shrink to ink, never grow; labels side by side move one by one;
    pushed by the width drawn, not the room; a label touching the one
    above moves by that one's growth. The flag is carried through
    _merge_paragraphs (it was lost there, so none of it applied).
    Ordinary pages place as before.
  - Windows panel came out below the bottom of the screen: placed again
    from GetCursorPos, MonitorFromPoint/GetMonitorInfo rcWork and the real
    GetWindowRect, by SetWindowPos, and once more 60 ms after showing.
  - Read-ahead retired (it was on in old settings): the Windows log showed
    a full 4K screen read every few seconds all day (2-4.5 s each, slower
    over hours, BitBlt failures after the screen locked) and again at
    every shortcut press, beside the snip's own reading - snips read in
    2.4-2.6 s (and fell back to PaddleOCR) where 0.2 s is normal. Nothing
    has used what it read since 1.49.0. preread.enabled() is False, main
    no longer starts it, the switch is gone from Settings.
  - Gates r29 pass (overlap 25, painted_over 32, too_small 66).

1.49.1

  - Run check on the Mac (1.49.0): Listing PASS 1.1 s via Groq; Article
    7.1 s and Labels 7.0 s via Gemini. Cause: the warm-up ("Hallo", 5
    chars) took 6.8 s with the model loading and set per_char to 1.36 s,
    so every later page was expected to take minutes offline.
  - offline_model.last_run {load, work}; translator learns per_char from
    work only, pages of SPEED_FROM_CHARS (150)+. online_first needs
    _fast_online_ready() (Groq key, not resting).
  - translator._misaligned: two or more words in a row in an answer that
    come from another line's original and not its own -> out of step.
  - Gates r23 pass.

1.49.0

  - main snip: preread.lines_at no longer used; _settle_detached stops a
    cancelled snip and hold-to-see's preparation and waits for neither.
    (_reuse_cancelled, a cancelled snip of exactly the same pixels, is
    kept: it is the same reading.) Hold-to-see still uses the key-press
    reading, with missing rows read again (below).
  - translator._hymt_with_help: no race. per_char EWMA from offline runs;
    expected = per_char x characters; online first only when expected >
    ONLINE_ABOVE (3.0 s), or for 30+ items on a processor without a
    usable graphics chip before the speed is known. Offline failing ->
    online; online failing -> offline. Log: "offline on N items (C
    characters): T ms" or "<engine> chosen for ...".
  - select_icon.mac_selected_text: AX switched on for the app, then the
    role under the press: AXStaticText/AXWebArea/AXHeading/AXLink -> icon
    at once; otherwise the selected text asked (0, 0.15, 0.5 s). Click:
    mac_selection_now() for the text, else copy.
  - selfcheck.py + Controller.run_check + Support "Run check": pages drawn
    with the system face at 16 pt x screen scale; checks read (>= 0.8
    similar), untranslated (para_text in the source language or equal),
    drawn (lines_covered vs not held), time (read + progressive <=
    budget.DONE_MS). Report in data_dir/selfcheck.txt.
  - Gates r22 pass.

1.48.53 (not released; in 1.49.0)

  - preread.lines_for: _unread_bands(crop, lines) - runs of 6+ rows with
    edge share > 1.5% that no line (+-2 px) covers - are added to the
    bands read again (reread_bands). Log of the 1.48.52 session: a snip
    "used 13 lines already read" where 18 were on screen; three
    paragraphs were left in German.
  - offline_model._SENTENCE_SPLIT: also after ".." or "…" directly
    followed by a capitalised word. A 257-character paragraph of such
    sentences took 4.4 s as one piece.
  - select_icon.Watcher(log=): "select icon: shown" / "not shown
    (reason)", a reason at most once in 10 s.
  - Gates r21 pass, totals as r20.

1.48.52

  - select_icon.mac_selected_text(pid, at): AXSelectedText asked at 0,
    0.15 and 0.5 s after the release (AXManualAccessibility switched on
    after the first empty answer); if still nothing, the role of the
    element at the press point (AXUIElementCopyElementAtPosition) - a text
    role returns True: icon shown with no text, click copies.
  - offline_model.translate_lines: units run longest first; per-unit
    timings, the slowest named in the "hymt:" log line. PIECE_CHARS 220 ->
    160. Gates r20 pass (overlap 26 -> 25, the rest equal).
  - main logs "overlay : N lines as M paragraphs (sizes)" per snip, to
    see a paragraph split in two without logging its text.

1.48.51

  - select_icon.py (new). Watcher polls the left button every 40 ms on
    the Tk thread (click_watch's backends); a gesture is a drag of 8+
    points or the 2nd/3rd click within 0.5 s and 6 points. Not offered
    when the press began on a Squint window (winfo_containing), while a
    snip or hold-to-see is busy, or when the app in front is Squint or
    the taskbar (platform_util.front_handle).
  - Is it text: macOS asks Accessibility for AXSelectedText of the
    focused element (ctypes, 0.3 s timeout, in a thread); Chromium-style
    apps get AXManualAccessibility once per pid. Windows: the cursor was
    the I-beam when the press began (GetCursorInfo vs IDC_IBEAM).
  - The icon: macOS, a non-activating borderless NSPanel (level 25, all
    spaces, no hide on deactivate) with an NSButton showing the 1024 px
    logo at 28 pt; Windows, an overrideredirect Tk window made
    WS_EX_NOACTIVATE | TOOLWINDOW | TOPMOST, the logo flattened on its
    own blue and rounded by DWM. Placed 10/12 pt right/below the pointer,
    flipped at the edge of the pointer's display. Goes after 4 s, on a
    press elsewhere, or when clicked.
  - Click: main._select_icon_clicked - with text (Mac) calls
    trigger_clipboard(given=text), which skips the copy and leaves the
    clipboard alone; without, platform_util.bring_back(handle) then
    trigger_clipboard() 150 ms later.
  - CFG "select_icon" (default on); Settings › Behaviour switch calls
    controller.set_select_icon.
  - Tap vs hold (macOS): hotkey_carbon installs kEventHotKeyReleased too;
    the listener prints "RELEASES" before READY and "UP <id>" on release;
    PynputHotkeyManager.released(hid) compares release and press times;
    snip_key_pressed decides from it first (let go -> tap, held at
    HOLD_SECONDS -> hold). Before, a tap with Ctrl+Cmd held >300 ms read
    "X down 0 of 11" and became "hold (X not readable)" - every press in
    one session.
  - ocr_vision._Gate replaces the lock round a whole recognise(): one
    request at a time, per pass; a reading not under ocr.quick_only goes
    before background ones, and a background reading skips its optional
    zh/ja/ko passes while a snip waits. Timing line adds "N passes,
    waited Xms" (ocr.reading_detail). Logs showed read 45561 and 34115 ms.
  - overlay.held_back: figures ending in . ! ? on the line under an
    unfinished sentence (same indent, normal gap) are not held
    (_ends_a_sentence_above).
  - identifiers._currency_back: a currency sign in the source next to the
    same figures in the answer goes back, unless the answer names the
    currency in words.
  - numbering: "dash" marker ("- ", "– ", "— " then text).
  - translator._left_behind: an answer >= 0.85 similar to its line counts
    as unchanged. _misaligned: equal line count but 2+ lines (1 in 3 or
    fewer) a quarter or four times their line's length (CJK weighted 3) ->
    treated as a miscount.
  - engine_label returns "" for "(none yet)"; OverlayWindow.set_engine;
    finish(engine=) on the window that showed the page filling in.
  - preread: _log_failure logs a repeated failure once per 10 minutes;
    a monitor of zero size is skipped (display asleep).

1.48.50

  - slant.py (new): rectangle() turns a reader's four corners into the
    text's own rectangle (start-top, end-top, end-bottom, start-bottom);
    PaddleOCR's unordered corners are read along the long side, left to
    right (downward when upright); Apple Vision's are already in reading
    order. Within SLANT 8 degrees of level, fewer than 3 characters,
    length < 1.2x thickness, or an upright CJK column: nothing changes.
    attach() stores line["quad"] as shares of the box, so moving or
    scaling the box (scale_lines, layout_region) moves the corners.
  - ocr_paddle / ocr_vision: attach corners. ocr_paddle.image_to_text puts
    slanted lines on lines of their own, ordered by where they start.
  - ocr.split_at_gutters skips slanted lines (their box is mostly page).
  - watermark: heights are slant.thickness; tilted + oversized alone is
    not a watermark (also needs faint or repeated).
  - overlay.paragraphs_for: slanted lines are paragraphs of one (labels).
    overlay.layout: slanted lines become turned blocks (turned_block):
    colours from the plainer band just outside the line (_turned_colours,
    via a QUAD transform to level), size 0.74 of the thickness, down to
    0.5 before the cover is lengthened along the slant; never past the
    capture (_room_along) down to min(min_size, 0.35 thickness). Shape and
    row centre are stored relative to the cover's top left, so code that
    moves covers moves them. rows=[] so the existing row loops skip them.
  - Drawn by overlay.paint (row rendered level, rotated, pasted),
    OverlayWindow._paint_blocks and XrayView._draw_blocks (canvas polygon
    + text with -angle).
  - main: the overlay-or-card check no longer counts overlay.held_back
    lines (figures, addresses) as wanted.
  - Measured: 11 slanted headings on a 2168x931 table, layout 64 ms;
    5 ms per slanted line.

1.48.49

  - ui_panel.MiniPanel: one canvas, 300 px wide; Snip and Text tiles with
    the shortcuts (shortcut_field.pretty), "Auto ->" and a language picker
    (tk.Menu: QUICK_LANGS, then all under "More languages"), status line
    from ui_panel.status_line (offline_model.download_state / ready),
    re-read every second; a gear for the window. Icons, arrow, chevron
    and gear are drawn, not glyphs: one geometry (ui_panel.ICONS),
    canvas shapes where ui_kit.VECTOR_PLATES (Mac: CoreGraphics, Retina)
    and elsewhere a 4x supersampled alpha mask scaled down with LANCZOS
    (icon_image), at the display scale. Plate border left to DWM when
    Windows 11 rounds the window. Closes on click_watch, Esc, or the
    icon (toggle; REOPEN_GUARD 0.4 s so the click that closed it does not
    reopen it). A button closes the panel before its action (120 ms).
  - ui_panel.place: under menubar.anchor() on a Mac; on Windows by the
    pointer, above it when the taskbar is at the bottom, kept inside the
    work area (SPI_GETWORKAREA). Mac rounded corners via systemTransparent
    on Tk 8.6; Windows 11 rounds the window (DWM corner preference).
  - menubar.start(on_click=): the status button's action goes to the
    panel on left mouse-up; right-click or Control-click shows the old
    menu (setMenu_ + performClick_, then cleared). If the action cannot
    be set, the menu is set on the item as before.
  - tray: a hidden default item calls controller.request_panel on left
    click; "Open Squint..." is the default only without it.
  - main: request_panel (any thread -> queue "panel"), show_panel,
    _panel_text (platform_util.bring_back to the app in front before
    trigger_clipboard), _track_front every 500 ms on Windows (the tray
    click puts the taskbar in front, so the last other app is kept).
  - platform_util.front_handle / bring_back: pid + NSRunningApplication on
    macOS; HWND + SetForegroundWindow on Windows, skipping the shell.
  - offline_model: the sentence-split pattern is a raw string
    (_SENTENCE_SPLIT); _units uses it by name, as it already did in effect.

1.48.48

  - overlay.carries_an_address: held back only when the addresses are
    >= 40% of the line or it has <= 4 other words.
  - overlay FONT_CANDIDATES["default"]: HelveticaNeue/Helvetica before
    SFNS, matching TK_FONT_FAMILIES; OverlayWindow._paint_blocks shrinks a
    block's font when Tk's measure of its widest row exceeds the cover.
  - clipboard.sequence on macOS: NSPasteboard changeCount, so
    selection.grab and copy_was_observed know whether the copy landed.
  - trigger_clipboard: after a failed copy, main.clip_is_stale (same change
    count as when the shortcut was last used, else same text) shows a
    message instead of translating the clipboard; _note_clip_seen after
    Squint restores the user's clipboard or auto-copies its answer, so its
    own writes never count as a copy made by hand.

1.48.47

  - ResultPopup._autosize: width from the text (east-asian wide characters
    count 2), 24-60 characters; height from Text.count("displaylines") at
    that width, capped at 2/3 of the screen (then it scrolls). Was: lines
    guessed from len() at up to 54 characters, max 18.
  - Translate-selection in place (app/inplace.py) off unless the setting
    clip_in_place is true: the screen under a selection is the highlight
    colour, which the cover took as the page's background.

1.48.46

  - Seven-language test (bench/matrix/run2.py): en, de, fr, es, it, zh, ko,
    every direction (42), 6 paragraph lines + 3 labels each from real
    pages, through translate_lines(blocks=True) and the real model: 378
    answers, all in the target language and script; numbers kept (the
    flagged ones were words - "1" as "one", 7,4 millones as 740만).
    Labels were the weak part: offline_model.translate_label asks short
    items with a fixed LABEL_CONTEXT in PROMPT_CONTEXT (the hint inside
    the plain prompt was itself translated); leak check on 标签/按钮/菜单/
    单元格 falls back to the plain request.
  - Pages into ko, zh-CN and de (bench.py BENCH_TARGET): zh-CN showed 17-24
    lines per page left in the original. overlay.split_over shares by
    characters when > 30% of the text is kana/Han, translate_grouped uses
    it, and layout keeps a line with an empty share but a translated
    para_text. After: 1-2 per page, as for other targets. English run r17
    vs r16: overlap 29 -> 26, too_small 74 -> 66; new gate baseline.
  - app/inplace.py: grab() takes the screen under the pointer at the start
    of trigger_clipboard, before any toast (the scan card shows the copied
    text and would be found instead); locate() reads an area of 1800x1100
    round the pointer, then the page in front, with the fast reader only,
    and find() takes the run of lines (one under another, overlapping
    sideways) that accounts for >= 60% of the copied text (whole lines, or
    >= 60% of a line at the start or end of the selection). App._snip_area
    (split out of _do_snip) handles it as a snip; "clip_inplace" on the
    queue; the card when not found, when overlay mode is off, or when a snip
    holds self.busy.

1.48.45

  - User log (1.48.44, 4K screens, zh/ja/ko/ru/de packs): read 5.9-9.0 s

    key-press read is unchanged. Downscaling for PaddleOCR measured: no
    gain (recognition per line dominates).
  - app/budget.py: READ_MS 800, FIRST_MS 1000, DONE_MS 3000, LAYOUT_MS

    _wrap adds known widths and measures whole only within 2 px of the
    limit; grow_to_ink and background_run read ink from the imaging

    by pixel. Same blocks on 7 pages (424 of 424), slowest layout on the

  - offline_model._pieces: a sentence over 1.3 x the piece limit is also
    cut after ";" (_CLAUSE) - not at commas, colons or dashes.
  - bench/gates.py (outside the app): quality totals not above the
    baseline, layout <= LAYOUT_MS x 3 on the 2-core test machine, no piece

    key-press reads 15-28 s via PaddleOCR (Korean), snips waited ~6 s for

    in prep_state, set by App._settle_detached for a snip elsewhere.
    ocr._looks_unread: the coverage test only below SHORT_READING 40 dense
    characters.
  - identifiers.find: a _CODE match must itself contain a digit (the
    look-ahead saw the digits of a following "[15]").
  - overlay._footnote_heights: footnote rule from 1.1 x the neighbour's
    height (was 1.2); a German paragraph with [11], [12][11], [13], [14]

  - User log, German page into Korean (1.48.44): key-press read 5.5 s (the
    background PaddleOCR reads were holding the processor), translate

  - ocr_windows._break_tie (_judge_part, choose_reading): level best ->

  - overlay._footnote_heights: a line of <= 3 characters shorter than its
    neighbour is compared at the neighbour's height.
  - offline_model.translate_lines (whole): an item longer than 1.3 x
    PIECE_CHARS 220 (PIECE_CJK 90) with >= 2 sentences is cut by _pieces

1.48.44

  - offline_model.auto_setup, from App._warm_reader at every start: when
    no model is here and offline is not declined (switch off or Remove),
    paused (Pause) or switched off after an earlier auto set-up, and the
    disk has >= 3 GB free, sets offline_enabled and offline_auto_done and
    starts the resumable download; self_test when it arrives. Logged as
    "offline    : <what it did>".
  - langid (new): in_language(text, target) True/False/None by script
    (kana vs Han, Ukrainian vs Russian letters, Persian/Urdu letters) and,
    for Latin text of >= 4 words, langdetect (seed 0, prob >= 0.9);
    page_in_language over the items in the target's script. The blocks
    wrapper in translator.translate_lines keeps an item as it is when it is
    in the target, or undecided, same script, on a page that is. Checked on
    the bench pages: 2 of 175 foreign Latin lines taken for English (a
    brand name, a list of console names), 0-1 for de/fr/es/it/pt.
  - Any-to-any matrix (bench/matrix): 6 source pages x 12 targets with the
    real model, every answer in the target language. offline_model.WRITES:
    the model's own list; sv/no/da/fi/hu/sk/el/ml/pa/tl came back wrong
    (wrong language, wrong meaning, or a loop). profile.translating drops
    hymt under Automatic for those; the backend walk drops it as the last
    resort; _hymt_beside does not run.
  - ocr.image_to_lines / image_to_text: a doubtful Windows reading was only
    passed on when cloud_readers() existed, so with no key PaddleOCR was
    never asked and the Latin-pack reading of a Chinese page was the
    answer. _second_opinion decides: "misread" (might_be_misread, empty, or
    a CJK source named with no CJK pack), "unread" (no CJK pack, source auto,
    and the reading is mostly 1-2 letter scraps or has a line on < 50% of
    the picture's text rows - _text_rows_covered), else None. PaddleOCR is
    asked for either; after "unread" its reading is used only if it has
    script characters or 1.3x the text (_paddle_better), else Windows'.
  - ocr_paddle.prefetch, started from ocr.warm on its own thread: builds the
    Korean engine (PP-OCRv5 mobile rec), which fetches its model once. The
    default multi PP-OCRv6 model in the reader's package reads Latin with
    accents, Chinese and Japanese (checked on the 20 bench pages: German,
    French, Spanish, Italian, Chinese recall 0.74-1.0), not Korean,
    Cyrillic, Greek or Arabic.
  - ocr_windows._is_jumble: vowels and consonant runs over all Latin
    letters with accented vowels as vowels (the ä of "Fahrradhändler" was
    dropped, leaving "dhndl"); "sch ch ck th ph sh tz" fold to one
    consonant; vowel share threshold 0.28 -> 0.15; case switches counted per
    hyphen part; accent+capital signal ignores a word's first capital.
    _judge_part dropped a one-word Latin part whose only word was "jumble",
    so German single-word labels vanished from the reading.
  - ocr_windows.latin_rank: Latin readings of one part tie-break on fewer
    letter-digit misreads ("0ber", "pers6nlich"), then more accented
    letters, before the tag - the tag made en-US beat de-DE on every tie.
    Used by _judge_part and choose_reading.
  - ocr.is_debris in _clean_lines: >= 3 marks, no number, no run of 3
    letters, letters <= half - dropped.

1.48.43

  - offline_model._launch passes --cache-ram cache_ram(): 1536 MB on
    >= 15 GB, 1024 below (llama-server's default 8192 grew until the test
    machine killed it). Model 1.1 GB + KV (SLOT_CONTEXT x slots, ~64 KB a
    token) + kept + ~0.3 GB compute stays under 4 GB. No speed change
    measured between 0 and 1024.
  - translator._hymt_with_help: help asked at once (wait 0) when the page
    has >= DENSE_ITEMS 30 items and offline_model.on_graphics_chip() is
    False - the first page used to wait HELP_AFTER because the offline
    speed was not yet known. Graphics-chip machines unchanged.
  - Speed: overlay.sample_colours uses a stepped point() LUT + getcolors;
    background_below uses ImageChops difference + getprojection;
    background_run no longer copies to RGB; the progressive painter waits
    max(PAINT_EVERY, 2 x the last layout time). Layout on a dense page
    2.4 s -> 0.9 s on the 2-core test CPU.
  - Test bench (outside the app): 20 real pages captured in a browser
    (screenshot + visible text lines), Squint's own _clean_lines /
    progressive / layout with the real Hy-MT2 on CPU, checks for text left
    showing, overlapping text, split words, untranslated text, covers
    painting over other content and tiny text. Overlaps 49 -> 26, covers
    over other content 113 -> 29.
  - ocr.split_at_gutters: also cuts a line at blank runs >= LINE_GAP 2.5 x
    the page's typical line height (_wide_gaps), and a menu row at runs >=
    MENU_GAP 0.6 x when every piece is a short label (_menu_gaps). _cut
    shares text over the written width only and cuts at the nearest space.
  - overlay: _LIST_MARKER and numbering "bullet" kind take ・●■▶›» etc.;
    group_paragraphs never joins two _short_item lines (<= 4 words and 32
    chars, <= 12 CJK); weigh_lines records ink_colour, and a colour change
    with >= 18% height change ends a paragraph.
  - overlay.layout: background_below caps each block's room at the plain
    background under it; a multi-row block shrinks (to min_size) to fit
    it; one row never shrinks for room. _faint_is_furniture treats a mark
    filling >= 60% of the line's own rows as a wall. Tiers are per column
    (cells and prose together). Lines taller than 2.5 x their width stand.
  - overlay.layout: cells reach right like prose (room_beside, now also
    counting lines left standing, minus COVER_GAP; background_run step 1
    so a 1px border stops it). _size_that_fits_wrapped and the final size
    step down until the longest word fits. Lines whose paragraph came back
    unchanged are left standing (not covered or redrawn).
  - overlay.grow_to_ink: box followed over ink starting within a
    letter-space of its left/right edge, up to 1.2 x height, ending at a
    0.4 x height gap; vertical version gives ink_bottom for the cover.
  - _unwrap_lists: a short line counts as wrapped when the next line's
    first word would not have fitted. cells_in: not a cell if prose
    starts right under it at the same indent, or if a same-size line sits
    within half a line above/below.
  - offline_model.LIST_ITEMS = 1, and translator passes no neighbour
    context: with it, 11 of 50 labels differed between a snip and the
    whole page; without, 0 of 50.
  - Snip key: "up" counts as a tap only once X has read as down (or with
    the modifiers up too). X never read as down with the modifiers held
    until HOLD_SECONDS is a hold. Each decision is logged ("hotkey : tap/
    hold after N ms (X read down a of b times)").
  - preread.read_now grabs the screen on its reading thread, not the Tk
    thread that polls the key.

1.48.42

  - overlay.progressive: translate_grouped with a progress callback;
    a painter thread lays out every PAINT_EVERY 0.35 s with finished
    paragraphs' text and the rest standing in with their own text, and
    shows only finished paragraphs (blocks carry "para"). Snip: queue
    "overlay_part" -> OverlayWindow.show_blocks, finished into the same
    window (finish) unless the page grew or codes were lost; closing it
    early cancels the snip. X-ray: partial frames through _post.
  - translator.translate_lines(blocks): needs_no_translation items kept,
    repeats sent once, progress(i, text), context (two items either side)
    via thread-local to offline_model.
  - offline_model.translate_lines(whole): consecutive short items (<= 3
    words / 24 chars, <= 8 CJK) in numbered lists of <= 8
    (translate_list, parsed and checked, else one by one); a lone short
    item with the model's own context template (translate_with_context, dropped
    if it echoes the context); items handed out in page order; progress
    per item; no left_behind retry (temperature 0).
  - _online_lines: > 40 items or 1,500 characters -> chunks, 4 at once,
    progress per chunk (_online_lines_one is the old single request).
  - Gemini: thinkingBudget 0 (2.5 non-Pro), thinkingLevel low (3+);
    dropped for a model that refuses it.
  - ocr_windows._reading_size: > 5 MP read first at half size with the
    first pack, kept if the typical line is >= 14 px; < 1.5 MP with a
    typical line < 11 px read at 2x. Boxes scaled back.
  - llama.cpp's flash attention is already on by default ("auto") in the
    pinned build; no flags changed.
  - ocr_windows.merge_readings: all installed packs' lines grouped by box
    overlap (> 0.5 of the smaller); per part, _judge_part picks the pack
    whose reading is in its own script (>= 2 native chars, >= 30% of the
    non-digit text; Korean only if plausible and > 1.5x the Han count of
    a Han reading of the same part), else the cleanest Latin reading
    (noise and < 60% reading score dropped). Replaces the whole-page
    choose_reading when more than one pack ran. ocr_vision: when the auto
    reading has lines in two or more writing systems, zh-Hans, ja-JP and
    ko-KR passes are added and merged the same way.
  - Snip key: the letter alone decides - up (read twice running) before
    HOLD_SECONDS = snip, down at HOLD_SECONDS = hold. HOLD_LONG unused.
    XRay._watch_key: X up closes at once if the modifiers are up, else
    after LOCK_GRACE 0.45 s unless X is tapped again (sticky).
  - translator.memory_tag (engine choice, background) is part of every
    translator cache key and the X-ray page key; config.set of a
    TRANSLATION_SETTINGS key calls translator.forget and xray.forget.
  - offline_model.SAMPLING: temperature 0, top_k 1, top_p 1, seed 1
    (was 0.2 / 20 / 0.6): the same text gives the same answer.
  - preread.lines_at waits (EARLY_WAIT) for a reading under way, as
    hold-to-see does, so snip and hold start from the same OCR text;
    only rows of lines cut by the box are read from the snip.


1.48.41

  - Cancel: Esc polled with keystate.is_down("esc") every 40 ms while a
    snip works (must be seen up first); label click; shortcut again.
    cancel_snip detaches the _SnipJob (not stopped), closes the toast,
    releases busy. Results of a cancelled toast go to _landing: handed to
    a live snip with the same job_key (image fingerprint, origin, target),
    else kept in _ready for READY_KEEP 300 s. A new snip with the same key
    is shown from _ready or picks up the running job; one overlapping the
    detached job's area >= 30% (or the page being prepared) waits for it
    (SETTLE_WAIT 20 s); elsewhere, the detached job is stopped
    (translator.set_stop / Cancelled).
  - Picker dismissed: XRay.prepare 350 ms later - capture, snip_area, read
    (reusing the key-press read), translate and lay out with
    translator.set_background (Hy-MT2 only, 3 workers, never online), into
    the X-ray cache; _wait_prepared makes a hold wait for it. X-ray let go
    early now finishes and caches instead of stopping.
  - preread.remember_snip keeps the last snip's lines as a page of its own
    area; lines_at tries it after the read-ahead page. Strict lines_for
    re-reads the rows of lines cut by the box instead of refusing.
  - translator.translate_lines(blocks=True): per-paragraph answers kept
    BLOCK_KEEP 600 s, only misses are sent. Only sentences (>= 6 words or
    >= 15 CJK characters) are kept, tagged with the engine that answered
    (last_backend); reused only when that is the page's lead engine
    (_lead_engine: profile order[0], hymt in background).
  - profile.translating(AUTO) with Hy-MT2 ready: ["hymt", "groq",
    "gemini", ...rest] for every job, keys or not. _hymt_beside returns
    None when hymt leads.
  - translator._hymt_with_help (from translate_lines): Hy-MT2 on the page;
    if not done after HELP_AFTER 2.5 s (at once when the recent seconds per
    item x items > HELP_FROM 3.5), _online_lines with no_hymt in a second
    thread; first non-None whole answer wins, the other is cancelled. Only
    when a Groq or Gemini key exists and is not in cooldown; else the old
    _hymt_lines route. Logged under "translate  :".
  - preread.changed_bands / widen_bands / reread_bands: rows that differ
    (1/4 size, level 24, getprojection) grown to whole lines; only those
    rows are read again. Reader._patch does it in the background (local
    only) when under BAND_LIMIT 0.5 of the page; lines_for does it at the
    snip instead of refusing on any difference. A log of an animated page
    showed 1,940 whole-page reads of ~6 s each, none reused.
  - translator.translate_lines: lines the online engine left empty no
    longer wait up to 6 s for the whole-page Hy-MT2 run beside it; that run
    is stopped and only the missing lines go to Hy-MT2 (_hymt_few, 2.5 s).
  - xray._work logs "let go before it was ready" with timings.
  - translator._hymt: text of more than one non-empty line goes through
    offline_model.translate_lines (sentence units, _share_out) instead of
    one PROMPT_LINES request that had to return the same line count.
  - overlay.group_paragraphs: a line never joins a group whose last line
    ends in 。；！？.
  - preread.lines_for logs why a reading was not used.
  - preread.read_now() on every snip-key press: grab now, then (thread)
    layers + OCR of content_box only (local_only), Page(early=True,
    box=...). Snip reuses it through lines_at (box must lie within
    page.box); hold-to-see waits up to EARLY_WAIT 6 s for it. Background
    reads also crop to content_box. Pages older than 120 s are not used;
    background pages are not used once read ahead is switched off.
  - Snip log: layout time and release-to-translation total.
  - overlay._misplaced_again: per block, answer > max(80, 4x source + 20)
    chars (8x for CJK) or empty for >= 8 chars -> translator.translate
    of that block alone; kept only if the new answer is not misplaced.
    _ENGINE_NOTE strips "[unclear ...]"-style notes.
  - overlay.layout: cover margin max(expand, 6% of line height).
  - translator._retry_left_behind and overlay._misplaced_again run their
    requests in parallel; the joined request and the retries are logged
    ("translate  : ...").
  - offline_model.translate_lines(whole=True) from overlay.translate_grouped
    (translator.translate_lines(blocks=True) -> _hymt_lines/_hymt_beside):
    one unit per block. _units had joined blocks without end punctuation
    (191 blocks -> 37 "sentences" on a car portal) and _share_out spread
    the answer across unrelated labels.
  - ocr_windows.might_be_misread: with 8+ words, a second opinion only
    when more than 1 in 10 words is a jumble (6 of 62 snips in the log
    went to Cloud Vision on German compounds).

1.48.39
  - app/preread.py: a daemon thread grabs the monitor under the pointer
    once a second (skipped while Squint is frontmost or read_ahead is off),
    compares a 1/8 thumbnail; after 1 s unchanged it runs
    ocr.image_to_lines on the whole monitor and content_area.layers, and
    keeps that one Page (image, monitor, scale, lines, layers).
  - Snip (Controller._do_snip) and hold-to-see use Reader.lines_for(crop,
    box): same monitor, crop pixels matching (1/4 thumb mean diff <= 1.5),
    and no line cut by the box (>= 90% inside), else None and the crop is
    read as before. A reading under way is waited for up to 1.5 s.
    Hold-to-see also reuses the page's layers (skips the AX walk).


1.48.38
  - xray cropped the capture to the content rect before reading; when the
    rect was wrong (a PDF preview gave one starting mid-window) every line
    it crossed was read in half and translated as a fragment, and the rest
    of the page was left alone. The whole monitor is read again and lines
    are kept by their centre (xray.inside).
  - content_area (Mac): windows of layer 0-19 count (Quick Look and other
    panels float above 0; menu bar and Dock are 20+). A content rect that
    does not contain the pointer is ignored in favour of the window.
  - content_area.layers(): monitor (visible frame / work area), the front
    window on that monitor (Mac: first CG window of layer 0-19, not ours,
    at least 200x150, overlapping the monitor; Windows: the foreground
    window if on the monitor, else the one under the pointer), and its
    content. A content rect under 35% of its window is ignored.
  - xray._work is now a snip of xray.snip_area(): content rect, else front
    window, else monitor. One ocr.image_to_lines on the crop, one
    overlay.translate_grouped, overlay.layout with the snip's defaults and
    size_policy floor, blocks shifted to capture coordinates and drawn as
    canvas items. Batches, groups override, reach=False, fitted_layout,
    already_in and whole-screen reading are no longer used by it. Result
    cached by (target, source, md5 of the crop reduced 4x).
  - translate_lines beside online: 3 workers (as before 1.48.34).


1.48.37
  - keystate (Mac) read CGEventSourceKeyState/FlagsState for the
    combined session state (0), built from key events delivered to apps;
    a key taken as a registered hotkey is not delivered, so with another
    app in front the letter read as up at once and a hold became a snip.
    Now the HID state (1). The tap/hold decision also asks the modifiers:
    snip only when letter and modifiers are both up; hold when the letter
    is down at 0.3 s, or the modifiers still are at 1.0 s.
  - Snip shortcut default T -> X (ctrl+cmd+x / ctrl+alt+x). config.migrate
    moves a stored ctrl+alt+t / ctrl+cmd+t once (flag snip_on_x_v1).
  - overlay.layout(reach=False): no block reaches right into empty
    background (pad_r 0, as for table cells); xray lays out with it.
    text_region had taken its right edge from the widest line on screen -
    the bookmarks bar - so prose spread to the screen edge.
  - content_area: Chromium browsers and Electron apps build their
    accessibility tree only when asked; AXManualAccessibility and
    AXEnhancedUserInterface are set once per pid so the web area can be
    found (the first look may still miss).
  - xray drops lines touching the content's top or bottom edge (cut-off
    lines read as nonsense).


1.48.36

  - Controller.snip_key_pressed: the snip shortcut polls the key every
    25 ms; letter up within 0.3 s = snip, still down at 0.3 s = hold-to-
    see (xray.press with the snip spec). While a view is up every press
    goes to it (second letter keeps it; a press closes a kept one). Key
    state unknown: snip, as before. hotkey_xray is no longer registered
    and its Settings field is gone.
  - xray laid out at points (1.48.34) to save time; on small print that
    meant a 7 px floor, ink/colour sampling on a quarter of the pixels,
    covers that missed the original, and a bitmap of text shown soft on
    Retina. Now as the snip: layout at capture resolution inside the
    text's region (layout_region, blocks shifted to page coordinates),
    drawn as Tk canvas items (tag "tx") over the frozen capture, sizes and
    positions divided by the scale. No bitmap paint per frame.


1.48.35

  - xray: activating the app brought the open main window forward for a
    frame. Its alpha is set to 0 before become_foreground_app and put
    back (lowered first) 300 ms after the previous app is re-activated -
    the same shield the clipboard card uses. The hold-to-see window is
    created withdrawn and shown only once sized and painted.
  - Layout inside the text's own extent (xray.text_region/paint_region):
    the full window gave prose free room to its right, so lines spread
    across the page at mixed sizes. Now laid out on a crop from the
    leftmost to the rightmost line, as a snip is.
  - content_area (Mac): the app is the owner of the topmost layer-0
    window under the pointer (CGWindowListCopyWindowInfo), not counting
    Squint - after a snip Squint was frontmost and the whole screen was
    used. The AX walk enters only containers (not toolbars/buttons), 600
    nodes / 0.5 s; if no web or scroll area is found, the content is the
    window below its top toolbars.
  - Offline: 16 slots made a 2-line request take 3.7-6.5 s (was 0.3 s):
    GPU saturated, and the Automatic background run submitted every unit
    at once so cancelling stopped nothing. slots(): 8 / 6 / 4.
    translate_lines(workers=) - 2 for the background run beside online.


1.48.34

  - app/content_area.py: content rect under the pointer. macOS: AX
    (ctypes) on the frontmost app's focused window: AXWebArea, else the
    largest AXScrollArea (>= 25% of the window), intersected with the
    screen's visibleFrame (menu bar, Dock); walk capped at 400 nodes /
    0.35 s. Windows: monitor rcWork; Chrome_RenderWidgetHostHWND child of
    the root window under the pointer, else its client area. Lines whose
    centre falls outside are left untouched.
  - Batches are translated with the whole-screen paragraph groups
    (overlay.translate_grouped(groups=)); table cells found on the whole
    screen are marked is_cell and honoured by overlay.layout. Regrouping
    each batch alone had merged a column of cells into one paragraph.
  - fitted_layout: min_size steps down (x0.85, to 60%) until no cover
    passes the content bottom.
  - Speed (log: 142 lines, 19 batches, read 1406 ms, total 9450 ms; the
    cost was one full layout+paint per batch at Retina size): the capture
    is cropped to the content before reading; after reading, boxes are
    scaled to points and layout/paint run on image.reduce(scale) - 4x
    fewer pixels (135-line bench: 3.4 s -> 0.9 s per layout); batches are
    2 paragraphs, then the rest in at most 3; every batch already back is
    laid out together. Log line now has first-paint time.
  - Offline: offline_model.slots(): GPU and >= 15 GB RAM -> 16, GPU ->
    10, processor -> 4 (was a fixed 3); 1536 tokens of context per slot
    (was 2048). Log: 12.7 s of engine work finished in ~5 s wall with 3.
    The per-call pool follows the server's slot count; xray runs all (at
    most 4) batches at once. hymt log line reports the slot count.
  - xray.already_in: for an English target, lines with >= 3 ASCII words
    of which >= 25% are common English words are not translated.
  - Focus: the frontmost app is recorded before activation and
    re-activated on close. ::tk::mac::ReopenApplication/OpenApplication
    ignored while hold-to-see is up or closed < 1.5 s ago.


1.48.33

  - app/xray.py: hold-to-see. Captures the monitor under the pointer
    (mss), shows it frozen in a borderless topmost window, reads it once,
    orders paragraphs by column then top (overlay.columns), and translates
    in batches (first 2 paragraphs, then 6) on a 3-worker pool, shown in
    order. Each step re-runs overlay.layout/paint over all batches so far.
    Batches cached by (target, source, line texts); paragraph ids offset
    per batch so layout keeps them apart. Unfinished batches complete
    after release and are cached.
  - app/keystate.py: key and modifier state (GetAsyncKeyState;
    CGEventSourceKeyState / CGEventSourceFlagsState) polled every 40 ms.
    The view stays while X or any modifier is down. A second hotkey event
    after X was seen up freezes it (Ctrl+Alt+X X); events while X never
    came up are auto-repeat and ignored. Unknown key state: stays up until
    Esc/click/shortcut.
  - hotkey_xray (default ctrl+alt+x, Option on Mac) registered beside the
    other two; third Settings field; apply_hotkeys takes it.
  - Settings > Behaviour: the "Welcome screens / Show again" row and
    MainWindow._show_first_run_again removed; the last switch row now
    closes the card.
  - platform_util.set_dock_icon: NSApplication.setApplicationIconImage_
    with assets/icon.png at start, so the Dock shows Squint's icon even
    when the launcher fell back to .venv/bin/python3.


1.48.32

  - squint.log showed exe .venv/bin/python3: the launcher's check of
    Contents/MacOS/Squint-python failed, so the unpacked interpreter ran
    and TCC attributed Squint to "python3.12". Likely cause: the older
    python-build-standalone binary finds libpython via ../lib, which does
    not exist beside the bundle copy. Launcher now exports
    DYLD_FALLBACK_LIBRARY_PATH=.venv/lib (no hardened runtime, so dyld
    honours it) and logs the check's output; make_dmg.check_inner runs the
    same check at build time and warns loudly.


1.48.31

  - Start failed on Tk 8.6: PIL.ImageTk imports _imagingtk, which looks
    up Tcl/Tk functions by name; with Tcl/Tk linked statically into
    _tkinter they are not exported, and the import raised TypeError.
  - app/tk_images: wraps ImageTk.PhotoImage.paste; the first failure
    switches to feeding Tk PPM (raw bytes; 8.6 rejects base64 PPM) or PNG
    (base64) data. Installed at the top of main().
  - mac_toolkit.undo_after_crash: when start fails with a Tk or image
    error while the .tk9 backup exists, restore it and set
    tk86_swap_failed. Called from main()'s start-failure handler.


1.48.30

  - Crash report: EXC_BREAKPOINT in CFRelease from TKContentView
    resetTkLayerBitmapContext on a display reconfiguration, in
    libtcl9tk9.0 (python-build-standalone 3.12 for macOS ships Tk 9.0).
  - app/mac_toolkit: on the Mac app with Tk >= 9, stream a 3.12
    install_only archive from a pre-Tk-9 python-build-standalone release
    (TAGS), keep only _tkinter, libtcl8.6/libtk8.6 and their script
    folders, place them by rename (the loaded Tk 9 files are never written
    over), keep the Tk 9 _tkinter as .tk9, and check a child Python gets
    TkVersion < 9. Anything else is undone; tk86_swap_failed stops retries.
    Started from Controller._warm_up; the header offers a restart.
  - app/__init__: sets TCL_LIBRARY/TK_LIBRARY for the Mac app's venv when
    it uses Tk 8.6, before tkinter is imported (8.6 reads its scripts
    from disk; the interpreter runs from inside the app bundle).
  - tools/make_dmg.command: the runtime must have lib/tk8.6; tries the
    TAGS releases in order. Older builds link Tcl/Tk 8.6 statically into
    _tkinter and ship only lib/tcl8.6 and lib/tk8.6 (no libtk8.6.dylib),
    so the folder, not the dylib, is what marks Tk 8.6 (also in
    mac_toolkit._fetch and already_swapped).
  - DisclosureRow: no hand2 cursor (it stuck under Tk 9 on macOS).
  - Release zip now includes tests/. The earlier suite was lost with the
    workspace; tests/ starts again from here.

    in a background thread (_hymt_beside) and runs the online pass with
    _job.no_hymt; online result wins; on TranslationError the local result

1.48.19

  - offline_model: Compact (2-bit) removed; stock llama-server cannot load

    offline_model.translate_lines. Groups of <=6 lines / 400 chars,
    run on a 3-worker pool against llama-server -np 3 (-c 6144); a group
    with the wrong line count is redone per line in the pool; up to 6
    left-behind lines retried alone. Was: one joined request, then every
    line serially, then up to 4 serial retries.

    logical. max_tokens 32 + 2*len, capped 1024 (was up to 2048).

1.48.16

  - offline_model: Hy-MT2 1.8B GGUF from Hugging Face (Q4_K_M, 1.13 GB, or
    Tencent's 2-bit file, 601 MB), resumable with Range into a .part and
    checked against the LFS sha256 from the HF tree API; llama.cpp's latest
    prebuilt release for the platform (win-cpu-x64, macos-arm64/x64) from
    the GitHub API, with the MSVC runtime DLLs copied beside it on Windows.
    All under data_dir()/offline. llama-server runs on 127.0.0.1 on a free
    port, started on first use and stopped after 10 minutes idle and at
    exit. Tencent's prompt, no system prompt, temperature 0.2.

  - Tencent's README says its small GGUFs need an STQ kernel from an
    unmerged llama.cpp PR (ARM only so far). If the 2-bit file is one of
    them, llama-server exits on load and the error says to choose Standard.

  - Backend "hymt": in _GOOD after groq, in _FAST after the free Google
    endpoints; the LOCAL profile is ["hymt", "offline"] with no fallback.
    Settings: the Offline translation card moves under Engines and leads
    with the Hy-MT2 switch, size choice, progress, Pause/Resume and Remove.


1.48.15

  - The installer's Python is the embeddable one; its ._pth file hides the
    isolated build environment pip makes for a source package, so any
    sdist fails with BackendUnavailable. omegaconf (via rapidocr) needs
    antlr4-python3-runtime 4.9.3, sdist only. A wheel of it ships in
    assets/wheels; install_requirements passes --find-links there and
    --prefer-binary, and on a build failure installs setuptools and wheel
    and retries with --no-build-isolation.

  - The update reloads the updater module from the unpacked build before
    installing packages, so a fix to installing applies to the update
    that brings it. The failure message says to reopen Squint (the start-
    up repair finishes it) instead of running setup, which on an installer
    copy never installed packages.


1.48.14

  - ocr_paddle: RapidOCR (PaddleOCR models, ONNX Runtime) on Windows,
    after Windows OCR and before the cloud readers, in image_to_lines and
    image_to_text. The default model reads zh/ja/en; Korean's model is
    fetched on first use when the source is ko, or when the default
    reading is empty or looks misread. Warmed at startup. requirements.txt
    adds rapidocr and onnxruntime for win32 (about 200 MB installed, most
    of it OpenCV and ONNX Runtime; about 75 MB more in the installer).

  - Settings: _key_row per key (dot, field, door, How?); Gemini and Groq
    in front, DeepL and Google in a frame under an Advanced translation
    toggle (show_advanced_keys, None until first used, then open when a
    Google or DeepL key is saved). The Translating list hides Google and
    DeepL the same way unless chosen or keyed. guide-groq.webp added.

  - translate_lines: after a joined answer with the right line count,
    _retry_left_behind asks again, one at a time and at most four, for any
    line an engine returned identical to its source (three or more words,
    detected language not the target) or empty. Seen with Groq: headline
    and paragraph together, headline translated, paragraph returned in
    German; the paragraph alone translated fine. looks_untranslated only
    catches a script mismatch, so German to English slipped past.

  - Controller._keep_main_window_back: on macOS, mapping the toast or the
    card activates the process and every window of it comes forward. When
    another app was frontmost as the clipboard shortcut was pressed, the
    main window is lowered now, after 60 ms and after 250 ms.

  - ScanToast.close hides every window at alpha 0 and destroys them 80 ms
    later; destroyed at once, a band could stay composited on macOS.

  - Test connection runs check_keys as well as the network check and paints
    each key's dot; verdicts are kept in key_checks against the key's last
    six characters (never the key) and shown again when Settings opens. Key
    rows and the Translating list are alphabetical.


1.48.13

  - translator._groq: OpenAI-style chat completions at api.groq.com with
    the same prompt as Gemini, temperature 0, through http_pool. The model
    is taken from the key's /models list by GROQ_PREFERRED then family,
    remembered in groq_model, and a decommissioned one is walked past.
    Qwen3 is sent reasoning_effort none and gpt-oss low (retried without
    it on a 400 that names reasoning); <think> blocks are stripped. A reply
    with a different line count is refused, as for Gemini.

  - Placed after gemini in BACKENDS, so with a Gemini key it runs in the
    background of the race; in EXPENSIVE, so a long snip never becomes one
    request per line against its per-minute limit. Profile GROQ pins it.
    Settings has a Groq key row (Get a Groq key opens the keys page) and
    check_keys tests it. No screen reading: none of Groq's current models
    take images.


1.48.12

  - google_billing: a free-trial billing account closes after 90 days and
    Google answers every request with a billing sentence. translator
    (_attempt, and the one-request Google path) and ocr_google report each
    Google failure; one that names billing queues a notice, at most once a
    day, withdrawn by the next success. Controller polls pending() every
    10 s on the Tk thread and opens ui_billing.BillingNotice.

  - Reminder: the date a key is first seen (whichever screen saved it) is
    stored with its last six characters; after 75 days, once, unless
    google_upgraded. A different key starts the count again. For keys that
    existed before this build the count starts on the first run of it.

  - ui_setup: rows in console order - project and card (judged from what
    the APIs say), Translation, Vision, key, upgrade (a switch the user
    sets, since a key cannot see the account's trial state). check() and
    its three results are unchanged. assets/guide-google.webp replaced.

  - ui_setup.GoogleSetup: a card above the steps with a thumbnail of
    guide-google.webp and "Open picture guide", which calls

1.48.10

  - ocr_windows.choose_reading ranked readings on script_chars — any CJK
    character counted — so a Korean pack reading a large Chinese headline
    as Hangul lookalikes tied with or beat the Chinese pack, and the page
    was translated as Korean ("ko → English", "no juice fraction").
    native_chars counts only a reading's own script (Hangul for ko, Han
    for zh, kana plus half the Han for ja), and plausible_korean sets aside
    a Korean reading whose syllables are mostly ones running Korean hardly
    uses, when another reading has its own script.

  - translate_pictures defaults to True and a saved False is moved once
    (pictures_default_v2): there was never a switch for it, so text on
    photos always went to the card.

  - translate_lines: with a Google key, the lines go as separate q items
    in one request (_google_api_lines), one answer each. From the log: a
    14-line snip made 14 requests after the joined request came back with
    a different line count.

  - _run_backends asks resting backends once when nothing else answered,
    not only when every backend was resting.


1.48.9

  - Every snip failed without asking anything.

    A user's error card: "Tried 7 — google-api-key: rested after HTTP
    400; … gtx: rested after HTTP 429; …". Each backend that failed was
    put in _cooldown for 60 or 600 seconds, and a resting backend was
    skipped outright — so on an office network where the free hosts
    answer 429, the first failures put everything to rest and every snip
    after that failed in milliseconds. Until 1.48.8 MyMemory, last in the
    chain and never rate-limited, answered and hid it. Now: a 400 does
    not rest a backend (it is about the request, not the service); and if
    every candidate was skipped as resting, each is asked once regardless.

  - _describe includes the service's own message from an HTTP error body
    ("HTTP 400 (API key not valid. Please pass a valid API key.)").

  - profile.translating: with a Gemini key, the SPEED order (snips) puts
    Gemini first, so snips get the race added in 1.48.8. It used to put
    Gemini last, which meant it was never raced and almost never used.


1.48.8

  - Gemini is raced against Google, with a three-second head start.

    Gemini was first when chosen, and Google was asked only after it had
    failed or timed out, so a slow Gemini added its whole wait to Google's
    and a snip could take ten seconds and still end at the plainest
    service. `_race_gemini` asks both at once: Gemini's answer is used if
    it comes within GEMINI_HEAD_START, otherwise whichever usable answer
    comes first. A Google win does not reorder the chain away from Gemini
    for the next request. When Gemini's answer is unusable to the caller
    (it merged lines of a snip), Google's answer from the same race is kept
    briefly and handed to the retry that avoids Gemini, rather than asking
    again. MyMemory, Lingva and SimplyTranslate are LAST_RESORT: out of the
    automatic chain, used only when chosen by name — one of them turned a
    Chinese headline into "without juice".

  - The HTTP pool: a POST reuses only a connection idle under five seconds;
    a GET on a reused connection gets two seconds to start answering.

  - Reading: a full-width colon or dash between digits on a CJK line is
    made ASCII, so the time and number repairs see it; 一/二/三 read as a
    dash, as their radicals or as = before a 、 are taken as the numerals.

  - ScanToast: the snip indicator is the snipped area, tinted, with a
    sweep and a stage label, built from several windows because a window
    can be translucent as a whole but not in parts. It falls back to the
    small pill where windows cannot be see-through.

  - Every snip logs one timing line: read time, line count, capture size,
    the language it was read as, translate time, the engine, the network.

  - Settings, Updates: "1.48.7 → 1.48.8" beside the install button, which
    takes the check button's place; the notes wrap to the card.


1.48.7

  - A connection a firewall had forgotten was waited on for the whole
    timeout.

    A user's log: first snip 16 seconds, and "1 held (avg 10853ms)" — one
    request sent on a kept-alive connection that never answered. Some
    networks drop an idle connection without a reset to either end, so the
    request is written, nothing refuses it, and nothing comes back. The pool
    already retried on a fresh connection, but only after the caller's full
    timeout. A reused connection now gets REUSED_GRACE (2 seconds) to start
    answering a GET before the request is sent again fresh; a translation
    GET answers in a few hundred milliseconds and can be repeated safely. A
    POST keeps the full timeout — it may be a language model thinking, and
    must not be sent twice. Connections are kept for 20 seconds idle rather
    than 45. Tested against a server that answers once and then goes silent
    on the same connection: 10 seconds became under 3.5.

  - HeroBanner._step could run before the first layout set _box, which
    logged an AttributeError at startup. It now waits for the layout.


1.48.6

  - Squint installs on Windows from one file, Python included.

    The installer carries the official embeddable CPython 3.12 with Tk
    added, and every package already installed and compiled, into
    %LOCALAPPDATA%\Programs\Squint — per user, no administrator rights.
    The layout is the one setup.bat makes, with Python itself in .venv, so
    the updater and the scripts in the folder work unchanged; those scripts
    now accept either layout. Compiled files are hash-checked, so a new
    machine compiles nothing on first run. As its last step the installer
    loads every library once (tools/prewarm.py), bounded in time so one
    misbehaving library cannot hang an install, and with the operating
    system's text recognition last. It registers start at sign-in the
    way the Settings switch does, and that switch now reads the real entry.

  - The Mac equivalent is a disk-image build (tools/make_dmg.command) run
    on a Mac. The app bundle never changes: it unpacks its code and Python
    to Application Support and updates happen there. The offline engine
    is no longer listed in requirements.txt for macOS, because the package
    check would otherwise download several gigabytes behind a disk-image
    install; setup.command installs it itself, as before.

  - Settings: Updates first; the glossary card hidden (the glossary still
    applies); defaults for new installs are light mode, start at sign-in,
    and copy-to-clipboard and detected-text off. Start at sign-in on by
    default is made true at launch when nothing is registered yet.


1.48.5

  - A list marker is not language, and the engine read it as language.

    A Chinese notice numbered its points "1、" "2、" and its sections
    "一、" "二、" "三、". Translated into Korean and English in one
    request, "1、" moved to the end of its sentence, "2、" followed by a
    noun was read as "two of" it — 、 is the Chinese list comma — 二 came
    back as "I.", and 一 and 三 were left untranslated in front of the
    heading. There is nothing to repair afterwards: the number is
    somewhere else in the sentence, or gone. `numbering` takes the marker
    off before the paragraph is sent and puts it back in front of what
    comes back. Section numerals become Roman in a target that does not
    read Chinese; Chinese and Japanese get the marker back unchanged. A
    number the engine prefixes anyway is dropped only if it is the same
    number, so a sentence that starts with a number keeps it.

  - A phone number in brackets was reported missing while in plain sight.

    "（010-12345678）" came back as "(010 - 12345678)로". The repair
    compares runs of words, and the words were "…(027", "-" and
    "12345678)로" — the number glued to the Korean on both sides, so
    nothing looked like it. A number is its digits in order, and it is
    now found by exactly that. Separately, a reader that returns one box
    per word returns the hyphen as a word of its own; on a CJK line the
    spaces it adds between digits are closed up before anything reads the
    text.

  - Times kept the engine's spacing: "20: 00 - 23: 00".

    Times stay out of the codes on purpose — a target may write them its
    own way. A space after the colon is not a way of writing a time, so
    that is closed up, and a range the source wrote tight is written
    tight. Korean dates written 09-18 go back to 9월 18일, the form the
    source used; Korean only.

  - A watermark stopped every line that ran into it.

    Two points on the notice wrapped at the exact place the watermark
    began, with the rest of the line empty. The reach to the right stopped
    at the first pixel that was not background. It now knows the text's
    own colour: anything that strong still stops it at once, and a faint
    pixel stops it only if its column is filled from half a line above to
    half a line below — a panel or a rule, not the stroke of a letter.
    Measured on the notice: 780px of reach became 1647, and the time line
    went from 0 to the page edge.


1.48.4

  - The first translation paid for everything the app had put off.

    Every expensive library is imported where it is used, which keeps the
    window quick to appear and moves the bill to the first press of the
    shortcut — the one moment somebody is waiting. Measured cold: the PDF
    library 3050ms, the translation client 790ms, imaging 550ms, the
    language guesser's first guess 290ms. Measured again once the
    operating system has those files cached, tens of milliseconds. That
    gap is why a newer computer felt slower: a fresh machine reads every
    file off the disk for the first time, and a virus scanner reads each
    binary extension once as it goes.

    `warmup` loads them on a background thread a second after the window
    is up, and opens the connection to the translation service so the
    first request skips the handshake. On the snip path, 846ms of loading
    becomes 2ms. It writes one line to the log with what each step cost.

    Two things were learned getting there. Warming the reader through the
    app's own reading pipeline cost thirteen seconds of a processor core,
    because that pipeline treats a blank page as a failed read and tries
    everything again; it now warms at the lowest level, about a tenth of a
    second. And the warm-up was first hooked into the window, which is
    built many times over — a theme change rebuilds it, the test suite
    builds it dozens of times — and the suite began dying with "async
    handler deleted by the wrong thread". A job that belongs to the
    process now starts in the process's entry point, once.

  - A background job could take the app down with it.

    The package repair ran on a thread whose function held the window.
    When the thread ended the window's last reference could go with it,
    on that thread, and Tk will not be torn down anywhere but the thread
    that made it — it stops the program instead. An install runs for a
    minute; a theme change rebuilds the window in less. `in_background`
    gives a job the window's queue and a weak reference, so a job can
    never keep a window alive. And the repair installs only into the
    `.venv` setup made, never into whatever Python happens to be running
    the app.

  - A paragraph kept losing its last line, which read as bad Korean.

    Reported by a Korean reader as a spacing problem, on a Chinese memo
    translated into Korean. The spacing inside the sentences was right.
    What was wrong was the end of every paragraph standing on its own, a
    word to a line. Two faults in the grouping, one after the other:

    Chinese is set tight, so each line's box runs a pixel or two into the
    next. The grouper skipped any line that started above the bottom of
    the one before, which was meant for lines sitting side by side on one
    row — and so nothing on a tightly set page joined. A sliver of overlap
    is now the next line down; half a line of overlap is still the same
    row.

    Where lines did join, a block whose first line fell short of the
    right margin was taken for a list and broken back into single lines.
    The short first line was the heading of the point, "2. 场景体验：…",
    and headings are short. A block where only the first line falls short
    is now a heading and a paragraph.

    Between them, each paragraph's last line was translated alone. It
    begins mid-word — "户个性化需求" is the end of 用户 — so it came back as
    a fragment repeating what the paragraph had already said, drawn in a
    box as narrow as that short line.

  - Three things the engine retyped that are not language.

    A seat layout, 2+2+3, came back as 7인승(2, 2, 3). Layouts are now
    codes, found even when written straight onto the next word, and put
    back by their digits when an engine re-punctuates them. Numbered
    points "1." came back "1,"; where the source point was numbered, the
    marker is restored. And a Korean particle came loose from the model
    code in front of it, "N90 는" for "N90는"; for Korean the space goes.
    Which particle is right is not guessed — it depends on how the code
    is read aloud — and 이, which is also "this", is left alone.

  - A test needed numpy, which this app does not.

    The measurement of the progress bar's rounded ends imported it, so on
    any machine where the app itself runs perfectly well the test errored.
    It reads pixels with the imaging library instead.


1.48.3

  - Typing one word removed every way of opening a file.

    The whole box is the file button, and it was unpacked outright the
    moment there was anything in the text field above it. The reasoning
    written down at the time was that a box with text in it is a box with
    text in it, and a dashed rectangle under the words invites you to do
    what you have already done. That is true of the invitation and not
    true of the button, and the two were the same object, so taking away
    the one took away the other.

    It shrinks now instead of leaving: an empty box gets the full
    invitation, a box with text in it keeps one line of it, which is
    enough to say a file can still go here and takes almost no room.

  - And clearing the text did not bring it back.

    The only thing that ever called the code that packs it was a key
    being released, and clearing is not typing. So the box came back only
    if you typed something and deleted it by hand; the cross cleared the
    text and left the file button gone for the rest of the session.

    The counter under the box had the same fault from the same cause. It
    is worked out from what is in the field and it was worked out only on
    a key release, so after clearing it went on reading "12 / 10,000" over
    an empty box. Both are worked out when the box is cleared now, which
    is the same moment everything else about it is.

  - A translation still on its way could fill a box that had just been
    emptied.

    The text translation had no way of telling whether the boxes were
    still about the text it had been given. It takes as long as it takes,
    and one that came back after the box was cleared wrote itself into it
    — so clearing appeared to half work, and only when the timing fell
    that way, which is the worst way for anything to fail: it cannot be
    reproduced on demand and so it does not get reported until it has
    happened a dozen times.

    Same answer as the file translation got in 1.48.1: the text has a
    number, clearing the box changes it, and a result that comes back
    carrying the old one is thrown away rather than written in. The
    machinery already existed for files; the text path never had it.

  - Seven tests.

    That the box is still there once there is text, that it takes less
    room, that it comes back when the text goes, that the counter goes
    back with it, that a translation still in flight cannot fill a
    cleared box, that one still wanted does arrive, and that typing while
    a file is being translated does not disturb the bar reporting on it.


1.48.2

  - Drag and drop, third attempt, and the first two failed for two
    different reasons that looked like one.

    The extension was added to requirements.txt in 1.48.0. Updating from
    1.47.x ran 1.47.x's updater, which on a changed requirements file
    printed "run setup once after it restarts" and restarted four hundred
    milliseconds later — so nothing installed it. 1.48.1 fixed that
    updater, and then failed for the opposite reason: by 1.48.1 the
    requirements file had stopped changing, so the check said there was
    nothing to do and skipped the install that would have fixed it. A
    machine in that state could not be repaired by any further update,
    because the thing being checked had already settled.

    The check was the wrong question. "Has the list changed" is not "is
    the package here", and only the second one matters. `missing_packages`
    asks whether each requirement can actually be imported — skipping the
    ones marked for the other platform, and mapping names where the
